Title: Origenis Opera Omnia, Tomus 3: in Evangelium Matthaei commentariorum (Commentary on Matthew, Greek books)
Creator: Origen; edited by Carolus and Carol. Vincent. De la Rue (Maurists), newly revised by Carol. Henric. Eduard. Lommatzsch
Publisher: Berolini (Berlin): Sumtibus Haude et Spener, 1834
Publication Date: 1834
Rights: Public Domain
Language: grc (Origen's Greek text); lat (Latin translation and series where the Greek is lost, and Lommatzsch's notes)
Rights basis: The item's metadata carries Public Domain Mark 1.0 (licenseurl creativecommons.org/publicdomain/mark/1.0/), and the imprint year is 1834, so it is out of US copyright by date as well.
Source: Internet Archive item origenous-ta-heuriskomena-panta-t.-3-1834, fetched from the item's own OCR text file on 2026-10-26 by the Library thread.
Provenance note: Host OCR text, unchanged apart from this header. The title page was read in the OCR, and the text was read at three points.
Content note: in Evangelium Matthaei commentariorum (Commentary on Matthew, Greek books). The OCR reads as Greek and Latin with occasional misreadings of accents and breathings and stray marks from the footnote apparatus. Check any quotation against the page image before it goes into a record. Volume 1 of this set (the first part of the Commentary on John) was not vendored: its host OCR is unusable noise.

In'his primum, quem Tibi nunc prae caeteris 
utilem judicabam, inspexi, atque, quantum ob tem- 
poris angustias fieri potuit, cum editione fratrum 
de la Rue comparavi, ut saltem specimen lectionum 
variantium "Tibi propinare possim, In eo multae 
voces sunt decurtatae, accentus ubique et recte qui- 
dem additi, sed in encliticis quaedam fluctuatio. 
Jota subscriptum saepissime deest, ac fere tüntum 
in Dativo Nominum in ὦ servatur, Interpunctio, 
quae dicitur, quodammodo arbitraria est, atque so- 
lis commatibus et punctis constat. Haec in univer- 
sum praemittenda videbantur, reliqua omnia, eaque 
etiam, quae minoris momenti sunt, inter variantes 
enumerafa reperies. In his enim, quae comparavi, 
ne jota quidem omittendum putavi. 


Atque haec fere habui, quae de Codicibus Ori- 
genianis Tibi scriberem: ac spero fore, ut in edendo 
Origene ex his aliquam utilitatem ac fructum hau- 
rire possis. De vita, quae hic vivitur, deque Ar- 
meniis meis, qui summa comitate ac benignitate 


me receperunt, alio ree. ubi plus otii erit,
